# 第 12 章　落木逐火英雄纪

*3.0 版本主视觉　·　图源：米游社*

> Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase　·　落木逐火英雄纪

## 095　Silver Chariot, Away to that Blackened Land　银辇啊，迅赴那黑色大地

> 世界：Astral Express　|　区域：Parlor Car　|　角色：Trailblazer; Pom-Pom; March 7th; Dan Heng; Himeko; Welt; Black Swan; Sunday; Tribbie; Trianne; Trinnon; Phainon; Aglaea; Mydei; Mem; Nikador; Castorice; Chartonus; Noldus; Virtus　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''A New Venture on the Eighth Dawn'' completed

### Receive a message from The Astral Express Family

### Join the navigation meeting to decide the next destination of the Express

> *The journey to Penacony has come to a close once again. With the Express running low on energy, a journey that is sufficiently Trailblazing is urgently needed to refuel.*

[Recap] Conductor Pom-Pom called a navigation meeting where, after discussion and voting, the Crew decided the Express would next head to Amphoreus.

**(Trailblazer):** (Now that everyone's here, I should go join them.)

**(Trailblazer):** (Hmm? It's Pom-Pom. Time for our navigation meeting?)

- ▸ Join the navigation meeting

**Pom-Pom:** Please stand by, passengers. The navigation meeting will be starting soon!

> **帕姆：**各位乘客请稍候，航线会议马上开始。

*(The first two choices disappear after choosing them once)*

- ▸ Black Swan isn't joining us?

**Himeko:** She doesn't think it's right to join in on our vote when she's only hitchhiking with us, so she's waiting in the Party Car.

> **姬子：**她说自己只是个搭车客，不该干涉航路去向，会在派对车厢等待结果。

**Himeko:** Though she did say that she believes we'll end up deciding on Amphoreus.

> **姬子：**另外，那位忆者还补了句——相信各位一定会选择「翁法罗斯」。

- ▸ She can read our minds!
- ▸ Let's prove her wrong.

**Himeko:** I'm sure everyone has a destination in mind.

> **姬子：**呵呵，大家心里自有答案。

- ▸ Where's Sunday?

**Welt:** He isn't joining us for the navigation meeting. He went to the Party Car to rest.

> **瓦尔特：**他无意参与航线会议，去派对车厢休息了。

**Welt:** Don't worry about him. An unexpected destination could be a great starting point for Sunday.

> **瓦尔特：**不用担心，对星期日而言，一处意料之外的目的地会是个不错的起点。

(If the player decided to reject Sunday during Trailblaze Mission A New Venture on the Eighth Dawn)

- ▸ I'm not worrying about him.

**Welt:** I can't decide if he'll be happy or disappointed to hear that.

> **瓦尔特：**呵呵，不知他听到这句话，是该高兴还是该失落呢。

- ▸ Send him back to the IPC, then!

**Welt:** That's unexpected, alright... but let's not do that.

> **瓦尔特：**那的确意料之外，还是算了吧。

- ▸ He's pretty laid-back about this, huh.

**Welt:** Laid-back? That's... not exactly a word I associate with him.

> **瓦尔特：**你会用这个词形容他，倒是在我的意料之外了。

(If the player decided to accept Sunday during Trailblaze Mission A New Venture on the Eighth Dawn)

- ▸ Let the control freak lose all control!

**Welt:** Your words, not mine.

> **瓦尔特：**呵呵，我可没这么说。

- ▸ Send him to a Washtopia!

**Welt:** That's unexpected, alright... but let's not do that.

> **瓦尔特：**那的确意料之外，还是算了吧。

- ▸ He's pretty laid-back about this, huh.

**Welt:** Laid-back? That's... not exactly a word I associate with him.

> **瓦尔特：**你会用这个词形容他，倒是在我的意料之外了。

- ▸ Let's start voting on our destination.

**Pom-Pom:** Since everyone's here, Pom-Pom will start presenting our options. First up is the oceanic planet of Lushaka.

> **帕姆：**既然大家都准备好了，我先列下几个选项：首先是「海洋星球」露莎卡。

**Dan Heng:** Mr. Mikhail's homeworld. Lushaka was hit by rising sea levels after the Stellaron Disaster, though I'm not sure how things are on the planet now.

> **丹恒：**那里是米哈伊尔先生的故乡。露莎卡曾受星核影响，全球海平面上升，不知现在变成什么样了。

**Pom-Pom:** Next, we have Melustanin, the Agate World.

> **帕姆：**然后是「玛瑙世界」梅露丝坦因。

**Himeko:** That's where Idrila the Beauty ascended, a world of undying allure. It's also said to be one of the initial sites of the Stellaron Disaster. Probably not the safest choice.

> **姬子：**「纯美」伊德莉拉的飞升之地，永存不灭之美的世界。据说那里是星核之灾的原爆点之一，多半不怎么安全。

**Welt:** Given the Express' fuel situation, it's worth us thinking through our options carefully.

> **瓦尔特：**考虑到列车燃料问题，这趟出行最好计划得周密些。

**Pom-Pom:** And finally, the most adventurous option: The Eternal Land, Amphoreus.

> **帕姆：**最后，也是最大胆的选项——「永恒之地，翁法罗斯」。

**March 7th:** A planet not recorded in the data bank and a world even Akivili has never set foot on... But if this trailblazing expedition works out, it could solve our fuel problem!

> **三月七：**阿基维利也没去过的世界，智库里一点资料都没有…但如果这趟「开拓」成功，燃料问题就解决了！

- ▸ The Express can soon achieve perpetual motion?

**Dan Heng:** It's not that simple. But we can save Pom-Pom from worrying about service delays, at least for a foreseeable period of time.

> **丹恒：**没那么简单，但至少短期内，列车长不用再为行程延误发愁了。

- ▸ We don't really have a choice in a time like this, do we?
- ▸ No one wants to go to Edo Star?

**Welt:** They suffered an ambush by the Antimatter Legion some time ago. According to the IPC, the enemy was repelled, but Edo Star placed their planet on temporary lockdown. I'm afraid there's nothing we can do there.

> **瓦尔特：**那里前不久刚遭遇反物质军团袭击。根据公司说法，敌人已经被击退了，但江户星暂时关闭了通行权。没什么我们能做的事。

- ▸ No one wants to go to a Washtopia?

**March 7th:** Geez, stop messing around. We don't need a scrub. The Express is already spotlessly clean.

> **三月七：**哎呀别捣乱了，列车干净得很，不用做大扫除啦。

**March 7th:** Amphoreus does have something to offer, unlike the other options...

> **三月七：**果然比起其他选项，翁法罗斯还是多了个非去不可的理由啊……

**March 7th:** Plus, it's a completely unknown world! What better place to trailblaze?

> **三月七：**而且，那是一个完全未知的世界哎，还有什么比它更适合「开拓」？

**Welt:** I feel the same. While we know nothing about Amphoreus, we know the other two worlds aren't exactly safe options. Anyway, if it's a risk we're taking, we might as well throw in some adventure for good measure.

> **瓦尔特：**我的看法也一样。虽说前路未卜，但另外两个站点也谈不上多么安全。既然都要冒险，不如大胆一点。

**Dan Heng:** Agreed.

> **丹恒：**同意。

**Himeko:** So, everyone's leaning toward the uncharted world? It seems we've got a crew of like-minded adventurers on the Express.

> **姬子：**果然大家都倾向星图中不曾描绘的世界啊，列车真是吸引了一群意气相投的伙伴。

**Himeko:** That includes me, too. I'm for Amphoreus.

> **姬子：**——当然，我的答案也是翁法罗斯。

**Pom-Pom:** Looks like Pom-Pom's voting plan won't be needed, after all!

> **帕姆：**看来列车长预先准备的投票方案，已经不需要了帕。

**Pom-Pom:** It's official, then. Our next stop is... Amphoreus!

> **帕姆：**那我正式宣布：下一站——翁法罗斯！

**March 7th:** Great! I'm looking forward to the conductor's jump announcement!

> **三月七：**好诶！那就等列车长的跃迁通知啦。

**March 7th:** I'll be in my room, freeing up some memory on my camera~! I'm going to have to snap tons of pretty pictures this time!

> **三月七：**本姑娘先回房间一趟，给相机清点储存空间！这回要美美拍照。

**Himeko:** Don't forget to share the good news with Miss Black Swan.

> **姬子：**也把这个「好消息」带给黑天鹅小姐吧。

### (Optional) Chat with your companions

**(Trailblazer):** (I'd better start packing up, too. Or should I go chat some more with the others?)

> **开拓者：**（我也去检查下行李吧，或者再和大家聊聊？）

*(Talk to Himeko)*

**Himeko:** Ah, (Trailblazer). This is your first time exploring beyond the astral charts, isn't it?

- ▸ It doesn't feel any different to me...

**Himeko:** Worlds that have been mapped on the astral charts also present new surprises from time to time.

> **姬子：**呵呵，开拓者，这是你第一次探索星图外的世界吧？

- ▸ Same for everyone else, right?

**Himeko:** Maybe not for Pom-Pom. And worlds that have been mapped on the astral charts also present new surprises from time to time.

> **姬子：**毕竟就算是星图上标注的世界，也会发生日新月异的变化。

- ▸ Are credits going to be useless there?

**Himeko:** That's quite the detail to fixate on. Perhaps you should be worrying about language barriers instead?

> **姬子：**也许帕姆不是？况且，就算是星图上标注的世界，也会发生日新月异的变化。

**Himeko:** But we'll be fine. Let's just say I have a backup plan. I'll reveal it when the time comes.

> **姬子：**竟然在想这么细节的事？也许你得先考虑下语言问题？

*(Talk to Himeko again, optional)*

**Himeko:** But we'll be fine. Let's just say I have a backup plan. I'll reveal it when the time comes.

> **姬子：**别担心。以防万一，我还留了个「后备方案」。先卖个关子，等需要时再揭秘吧。

*(Talk to Welt)*

**Welt:** A world yet to be explored by the Trailblaze... No self-respecting Nameless would turn that opportunity down.

> **瓦尔特：**「开拓」也未曾抵达的世界——对无名客而言，是个难以抵御的诱惑吧？

- ▸ An opportunity hard to come by?

**Welt:** Absolutely. The Astral Express has always traveled along the silver rail laid down by Akivili. We don't normally get to venture into a world beyond the knowledge of the Trailblaze.

> **瓦尔特：**当然。星穹列车一直沿着阿基维利开辟的银轨行驶，正常情况下，是没有办法前往「开拓」认知外的世界的。

- ▸ Hah! So I'm better than an Aeon!

**Welt:** This is Akivili we're talking about. In the first instance, every one of THEIR stops was a world no human had ever set foot on. The Astral Express has always traveled along the silver rail laid down by Akivili, so we don't normally get to venture into a world beyond the knowledge of the Trailblaze.

> **瓦尔特：**那可是阿基维利，祂的每一站都是人类未曾抵达的世界。列车一直沿着祂开辟的银轨行驶，正常情况下，是没有办法前往「开拓」认知外的世界的。

**Welt:** But this time, we might just get the chance to add a brand-new stop to the astral charts. I don't mind telling you — I'm a little excited.

> **瓦尔特：**但这一次，或许我们有机会在星图上留下一个全新的站点，我也有些摩拳擦掌了。

*(Talk to Welt again, optional)*

**Welt:** An unknown world brings unknown challenges. Exciting stuff.

> **瓦尔特：**未知的世界意味着未知的挑战，我也有些跃跃欲试了。

*(Talk to Dan Heng)*

**Dan Heng:** I can't provide many details about our destination this time. You can ask the Memokeeper if you want to know more.

> **丹恒：**这次的目的地，我提供不了什么信息。如果好奇，你可以询问那位忆者。

- ▸ Are you nervous, Dan Heng?

**Dan Heng:** Why do you ask that? Are you feeling nervous yourself? It'll be fine. Just trust the Crew.

> **丹恒：**怎么突然问这个，你很紧张么？别担心，相信列车组。

- ▸ Oh ho, the data bank's getting a huge update.

**Dan Heng:** Mhm, I suppose there's a lot to do. I'll disembark with all of you this time.

> **丹恒：**嗯，想来是个大工程，这次我会一起下车。

**Dan Heng:** We've never had this many passengers on board before... I'm still not used to it. Why don't you head to the Party Car first? I'll see you later.

> **丹恒：**列车头一回迎来这么多客人，有些不习惯。你先去派对车厢吧。待会儿见。

*(Talk to Dan Heng again, optional)*

**Dan Heng:** Why don't you head to the Party Car first? I'll see you later.

> **丹恒：**你先去派对车厢吧。待会儿见。

*(Talk to March 7th)*

**March 7th:** Hey, great timing! I just turned on my camera and was looking through some old photos.

> **三月七：**哎，你来得正好！我刚才打开相机，不知不觉就看起了以前的照片。

**March 7th:** Can I ask you something? What's your favorite part of a journey, from start to finish?

> **三月七：**我问你哦，一段旅途从开始到结束，你最喜欢哪个时候？

- ▸ The start of a journey, when we dive into the unknown.

**March 7th:** Then let's go snap some photos as soon as we arrive in Amphoreus!

> **三月七：**那等到了翁法罗斯，咱们一落地就去拍两张吧！

- ▸ The middle of the journey, when some gains are made.

**March 7th:** Then you're going to have to give me a nudge whenever there's something worth snapping!

> **三月七：**那你可得多提醒我，随时准备好按下快门！

- ▸ The end of the journey, when we wanna commemorate the adventure.

**March 7th:** Me too! This time in Amphoreus let's make sure we get a group photo that everyone loves!

> **三月七：**咱也是。这次翁法罗斯之旅，可要留下一张大家都满意的合照！

**March 7th:** Anyway, enough chitchat! I better pack some extra batteries in case we end up somewhere without power!

> **三月七：**哎呀！不能光顾着聊天了，我还得多准备几块电池，万一落在了特别原始的地方呢？

*(Talk to March 7th again, optional)*

**March 7th:** Enough chitchat. You should get packing, too!

> **三月七：**不闲聊啦，你也快去收拾收拾吧。

[Recap] During your talk with Black Swan, you discovered she already knew the Express's next stop was her recommendation, Amphoreus. She hoped this trailblazing expedition would uncover the forgotten memories of Amphoreus.

*(Talk to Black Swan)*

**Black Swan:** Ah, you're here. I hear the Nameless are going to Amphoreus. A very wise decision.

- ▸ Who did you hear that from?

**Black Swan:** I am a Memokeeper, after all. Memories don't lie.

> **黑天鹅：**你来啦。听说各位无名客选择了翁法罗斯，十分明智的决定。

- ▸ Why do you want to go to Amphoreus?
- ▸ Why is the Garden turning to us for help?

**Black Swan:** Truth be told, it's more of a personal request.

> **黑天鹅：**我可是忆者。记忆是不会说谎的。

**Black Swan:** "Memokeepers traverse the worlds only to collect precious memories." Have you heard that before?

> **黑天鹅：**确切地说，这是我个人的请求。

**Black Swan:** It's my duty to salvage unknown memories, and the Amphoreus that has been revealed by the Garden's mirror is like a treasure inside a display case — dazzling yet out of reach.

> **黑天鹅：**你是否听过这么一种说法：「忆者穿行诸界，只为搜集一切珍贵的记忆」。

**Black Swan:** I need a sharp nail to break through that glass, and that's where you come in. Only through trailblazing can we reach the heart of Amphoreus, cut through the thick mist, and bring the world's "memories" back to light.

> **黑天鹅：**打捞湮没无闻的「记忆」是我的职责，而忆庭之镜映照出的翁法罗斯…就像橱窗中的珍宝，闪闪发光，也难以触及。

**Black Swan:** I trust you won't disappoint me?

*(Talk to Black Swan again, optional)*

**Black Swan:** I trust you won't disappoint me?

> **黑天鹅：**你不会让我失望的，对吗？

*(Talk to Sunday)*

**Sunday:** Looks like you've decided on your next stop?

> **星期日：**看样子，下一站已经决定了？

- ▸ We're going to Amphoreus.
- ▸ We're going to a Washtopia.
- ▸ We're going back to Penacony.

**Sunday:** I see.

- ▸ You're taking it very calmly.
- ▸ No comment from you?

**Sunday:** Those who embark on a long journey simply need to "set off."

> **星期日：**踏上远行的人，需要的只是「出发」而已。

*(Talk to Sunday again, optional)*

**Sunday:** I look forward to the journey ahead.

> **星期日：**我很期待接下来的旅程。

### Pack up and get ready to set off!

[Recap] You went back to your room to pack up, preparing for the upcoming warp.

*(At the entrance to the Trailblazer's Room)*

- ▸ Pack up luggage

After much deliberation, you realize that insufficient preparation is as good as no preparation! Might as well toss your luggage aside...

Never mind. Having some preparation is better than none. Even formalities have their place.

### Take a short rest at the Party Car and wait for the warp jump

[Recap] The train's speakers carried Pom-Pom's announcement: "Attention, passengers. The Express is about to make the jump. Please be seated and hold on!"

**(Trailblazer):** (Looks like it's about time. I should go find a spot to take in the views during the warp.)

> **开拓者：**（时间差不多咯。去找个位子，看看跃迁风光吧。）

*(Interact with the highlighted sofa)*

- ▸ Begin Warp Jump

**Pom-Pom:** Attention, passengers. The Express is about to make the jump. Please be seated and hold on!

> **帕姆：**各位乘客请注意——列车即将跃迁——请坐稳扶好帕——！

[Recap] After the warp, there was still nothing outside the window. Black Swan activated her Memokeeper ability to reveal a panoramic view of Amphoreus for you.

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Black Swan:** There's nothing there...

> **黑天鹅：**什么都没有…

**Black Swan:** Confusing?

> **黑天鹅：**令人疑惑？

**Black Swan:** The answer lies hidden in the starry sky.

> **黑天鹅：**答案就藏在星空中。

*(Cutscene ends)*

[Recap] Through the window, you saw Amphoreus and learned that it was wrapped in "Erudition," "Remembrance," and an unknown third Path. Oddly, March 7th was behaving unusually and remained in her room.

**Black Swan:** Here it is — the isolated world that can only be seen through the Garden's mirror...

> **黑天鹅：**看吧，这就是那个与世隔绝，只能被忆庭之镜映照出的世界……

**Black Swan:** The Eternal Land, Amphoreus.

> **黑天鹅：**「永恒之地，翁法罗斯」。

- ▸ So blindingly bright...
- ▸ What celestial body is this?

**Black Swan:** No one knows. That's what you're about to find out.

> **黑天鹅：**无人知晓，这是你们将要揭示的答案。

- ▸ How did it suddenly just appear?

**Black Swan:** I used a little magic so all of you can also see what it looks like.

> **黑天鹅：**我施展了一点小魔术，让你们也能看清它的样貌。

- ▸ Woah. It's in the shape of an "8"!

**Black Swan:** As you can see, Amphoreus is surrounded by a mass of chaotic matter that conceals it from the outside world — nearly. Conventional interstellar travel can't detect its presence, let alone pass by or land on the planet.

> **黑天鹅：**正如各位所见，翁法罗斯被一团混沌的物质包裹着，难以被外部观测。普通的星际旅行无法意识到它的存在，更遑论经过和到达。

**Black Swan:** Yet the Garden, caught a glimpse of this place, and along with it, discovered ever-shifting Traces.

> **黑天鹅：**但忆庭窥见了这里，一并发现的，还有其中变幻莫测的命途行迹。

**Himeko:** "Three Paths interweave and fetter Amphoreus, co-authoring the fate of the world." Ordinary Pathstriders aren't known to leave traces in the mirror, so if what you said is true...

> **姬子：**「三重命途交织缠绕着翁法罗斯，共同谱写世界的命运」——按照你的说法，普通的命途行者不会在镜中留下痕迹，所以……

**Himeko:** Then this isolated star system must have been home to at least three beings comparable to Emanators.

> **姬子：**在这遗世独立的星系，诞生过至少三位堪比「令使」的存在。

**Black Swan:** I would even go as far as to say Aeons THEMSELVES.

> **黑天鹅：**甚至，可能是星神本人垂迹。

**Welt:** For a world to have nurtured such marvels yet still remain unheard of throughout the cosmos? Now, that is hard to fathom.

> **瓦尔特：**如此人杰地灵的世界，在寰宇间却寂寂无名。确实有些奇怪。

**Himeko:** We already know one of the three Paths to be the Erudition...

> **姬子：**先前，我们已经知道了这三重命途的其中一重是「智识」……

**Black Swan:** I can reveal the second. In fact, you've all seen it just now. It's the Remembrance.

> **黑天鹅：**而第二重——不必向各位隐瞒，就在刚才，你们已亲眼见证了它，是「记忆」。

**Welt:** I see. Then it makes sense that a messenger of the Garden would be capable of unveiling Amphoreus for us. What about the final Path?

> **瓦尔特：**难怪忆庭的使者能揭开它的面纱啊。那，最后一重呢？

**Black Swan:** Unfortunately, fate is keeping the third Path a mystery. I don't know what it is, either.

> **黑天鹅：**很遗憾，命运吝啬于展现它的底色，我也不知道第三重命途是什么。

**Black Swan:** Hidden beneath the brilliance of Erudition and Remembrance, yet possessing enough power to rival the other two Paths. It could be the Equilibrium, the Enigmata, or perhaps the Permanence? I have no clue at all.

> **黑天鹅：**它潜藏在「智识」和「记忆」的光芒下，与二者分庭抗礼。是「均衡」？「神秘」？还是「不朽」？我没有头绪。

**Black Swan:** This white ribbon of light encircling Amphoreus might very well be a product of the three interwoven Paths. Only when Pathstriders of the Trailblaze enter the center of the vortex will its true nature be revealed.

> **黑天鹅：**这条缠绕翁法罗斯的白色光带，也许就是三重命途彼此交织的结果。也只有各位「开拓」的行者能深入漩涡中心，看清它的容貌。

**Dan Heng:** Still, we know far too little about the planet.

> **丹恒：**话虽如此，情报还是太少了。

**Dan Heng:** What's more pressing is finding a suitable landing site. We could end up over an ocean, in a vacuum without oxygen, or worse yet, in the middle of a lava field...

You stare intently at the red clay in your hand, then look up to check the dromas' expression.

**Dromas:** ...Grumph?

> **大地兽：**…嗡嗡？

You see confusion... hesitation... and perhaps a hint of hidden defiance. The big guy looks down at you, as if to say, "Do you have the guts, little one?"

You've never been underestimated by an animal in your life — though your life hasn't been that long. Now, there's probably only one way to save your dignity...

You take a bite from the red cube. Next to you, the two spectators who witness this gasp in disbelief.

**Dan Heng:** *wince*

> **丹恒：**嘶……

**Castorice:** Ah...

> **遐蝶：**啊……

Castorice wasn't lying. The taste of this red clay is truly... bitter. It's a bitterness unlike any medicine. It strikes the softest part of your soul, making you question the very meaning of "Trailblazing."

Surprisingly, it isn't hard, but that's not necessarily a good thing. After chewing it a few times, the sticky flecks of redsoil stain your teeth. No matter how you use your tongue, you can't get rid of it.

**Dan Heng:** ...Drink some water.

> **丹恒：**…喝口水吧。

You hold the clean water in your mouth, trying hard to rinse away the remaining clay, but the bitterness lingers stubbornly...

The murals record in simple and delicate carvings the stories of Kephale creating life and building Okhema.

In the murals, Kephale bears the enormous Dawn Device on their back. Golden divine blood courses across their body, flowing to the ground from the left-hand side of their chest and spreading in all directions.

At their feet, the humans bathed in the golden blood work together to create a great city. This city is Okhema, the origin of Amphoreus civilization.

## 097　Night Stars, Accompany My Slumber　晚星啊，莫让我长夜独卧

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域：Vortex of Genesis　|　角色：Trailblazer; Dan Heng; Phainon; Castorice; Aglaea; Tribbie; Trianne; Mem; Mydei　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Distant Travelers, Listen to this World's Prayer'' completed

### Submit to Aglaea's interrogation — it's better to speak the truth

> *Aglaea's control over Okhema runs far deeper than we anticipated, and the previous incident has already reached her ears. This leader of the Chrysos Heirs revealed a cold and unyielding side, going to great lengths to reassess our intentions. After such perilous moments, we must reconsider the viability of our expedition in Amphoreus.*

[Recap] Those who tried to reach the sky would be punished by the Sky Titan. Because you revealed "the world beyond the sky," Aglaea interrogated you and Dan Heng in the Vortex of Genesis, with Castorice as your executioner.

**Dan Heng:** ...What is the meaning of this?

> **丹恒：**…这是什么意思？

- ▸ Why is this happening again?
- ▸ We've been made...
- ▸ As expected.

**Aglaea:** This is no laughing matter. It's an interrogation.

> **阿格莱雅：**这不是玩笑，而是一场审讯。

**Aglaea:** While the both of you were roaming the city, someone was paying attention to your every move. Unfortunately... you have broken your promises.

> **阿格莱雅：**两位在城中游荡时，有人始终在留意你们的行动。很遗憾…你们背弃了诺言。

**Dan Heng:** So, you are indeed aware of our transgression... However, we only did so to save a life.

> **丹恒：**你果然已经知道了…但我们是为了救人。

**Aglaea:** The holy city's fate is as fragile as thread. I must exercise prudence in order to eliminate sources of potential disaster.

> **阿格莱雅：**圣城的命运如细丝般脆弱。为剪除祸端，我必须审慎。

**Aglaea:** I will grant the both of you another chance to mend our broken trust. The golden thread shall judge on my behalf. If you're truthful, you will live, else... you will perish.

> **阿格莱雅：**我会再给两位一次机会，重新缝补信任的裂帛。金线会替我做出裁决，坦诚则生…欺瞒则亡。

**Castorice:** We meet again, distinguished guests.

> **遐蝶：**又见面了，两位客人。

**Aglaea:** Castorice will be your executioner.

> **阿格莱雅：**遐蝶会是你们的行刑人。

**Dan Heng:** Lady Aglaea, it is undeniable that we broke our agreement out of carelessness. However, you never explained to us how revealing that a world beyond the sky exists would lead to dire consequences.

> **丹恒：**阿格莱雅女士，我们因疏漏大意打破了约定，这点无法否认。但你从未说明泄露「天外之界」可能导致的恶果。

**Dan Heng:** What I want to know is, exactly how grave a crime have we committed?

> **丹恒：**我想知道，我们究竟犯下了多大的罪行？

**Aglaea:** A reasonable request. It is indeed my oversight for failing to tell you everything in time.

> **阿格莱雅：**合理的诉求。未能及时向你们道出全部，是我的过失。

**Aglaea:** There have always been people in Amphoreus who looked beyond the skies. Indeed, there used to be a usurper nation that spared no expense in constructing "skyships," with the desire to break through the blue sky and reach the stars...

> **阿格莱雅：**翁法罗斯从不缺少向往天外的人。曾有一邦僭主穷尽举国之力修建「天舟」，意欲突破苍穹、企及星空……

**Aglaea:** However, Aquila's divine punishment fell the instant the fleet of skyships took flight. An entire city-state, along with all of its subjects, was reduced to ash in that moment.

> **阿格莱雅：**但在天舟启航的瞬间，艾格勒的神罚降临了。一整座城邦，连同所有的臣民，于刹那间灰飞烟灭。

**Aglaea:** It's not that Amphoreus is unwilling to head toward the stars. *(Enter Tidal Bounty)*

The water returns to its serene state... How curious — it appears that you just have to bring Celestial Ambrosia back here next time to get something nice.

### Speak to Phainon about the voices of Titans you heard

[Recap] Phainon wanted you and Dan Heng to join the Chrysos Heirs and help defeat Nikador. If you decided to stay, Phainon and Castorice would try to patch things up with Aglaea. Dan Heng let you decide whether to stay or go.

*(Talk to Phainon or Castorice)*

**Phainon:** So, did you hear it?

> **白厄：**如何，你听见了吗？

- ▸ I did...
- ▸ I couldn't understand it...

> **白厄：**第一次听见泰坦之声时，我还是个只知挥剑的孩子，全然不知生命的意义为何物。

**Phainon:** To this day, I still don't know why the prophecy chose me, but I'm grateful for it... Without this journey, I might've already become a wandering soul on the battlefield.

> **白厄：**同样地，我至今不知道神谕为何会选中自己，但我得感谢它…若非踏上这段旅程，我大概已经变成战场上的孤魂野鬼了吧？

**Phainon:** Alright, that's enough of my story. I want to extend a heartfelt invitation to you: join the Chrysos Heirs, and aid us in hunting down Nikador and retrieving the Coreflame from the Titan of Strife.

> **白厄：**说回你们，我想郑重地发出邀请，希望两位能加入黄金裔的阵营，助我们一同讨伐尼卡多利，从「纷争」的泰坦手中夺得火种。

**Phainon:** If you still harbor doubts, I'll do my best to provide you with answers.

> **白厄：**如果二位心中还有疑问，我会尽全力解答。

- ▸ How are the Chrysos Heirs chosen?

**Castorice:** ...Unfortunately, the workings of the prophecy still remain a mystery to us all.

> **遐蝶：**…很遗憾，人们至今没能弄清神谕的规则。

**Castorice:** The only reliable speculation is that... it favors those caught in dire circumstances and bestows miracles upon them.

> **遐蝶：**唯一可信的推测是…它更青睐那些身陷极端境遇的人，会在他们眼前降下奇迹。

- ▸ Where are the other Chrysos Heirs in the world?

**Phainon:** Legend has it that the first Chrysos Heir was born millennia ago. Since then, the chosen ones began to walk this earth.

> **白厄：**相传，第一位黄金裔诞生在千年以前。自那之后，神选者就开始在世间行走。

**Phainon:** Then came the Chrysos War... A conflict backed by the gods, yet one that brought much bloodshed and claimed many heroes.

> **白厄：**然后便是「黄金战争」…那场战役背靠神明，却让凡人流尽了鲜血，众多英雄在此期间陨落。

**Phainon:** By the time of my birth, the tales of the Chrysos Heirs had become more like legends than historical accounts. If there are others called forth by the prophecy... I trust that fate will guide them to us.

> **白厄：**到了我出生的年代，相较一段历史，黄金裔的故事已经更接近传说。如果世上还有其他受神谕召唤的人…命运会把他领到我们面前的。

- ▸ How do you plan to fight Nikador?

**Phainon:** Armed with courage and unwavering resolve, our quest will begin as soon as Trianne locates Castrum Kremnos.

> **白厄：**勇气和决心都已具备，只等缇安老师找到悬锋城的所在，我们就踏上征程。

**Phainon:** Without a doubt, it will be a momentous battle to the death... Prepare yourselves.

> **白厄：**当然，此行注定是一场撼天动地的死斗…做好心理准备。

> **白厄：**前路艰险，我不会强迫你们。但如果二位决定留下，我——哀丽秘榭的白厄，定会努力修补你们和黄金裔之间的嫌隙，不遗余力。

> **白厄：**请一定要在浴宫中好好休息。等两位蓄满精力，相信缇安老师也带着悬锋城的消息归来了。

(Same dialogue as above)

> **白厄：**早些时候，你们对我的苦衷施以理解，现在该我做一样的事了。

> **遐蝶：**黄金裔的旅程…果然很孤独啊。

**Phainon:** Let's go, Dan Heng, (Trailblazer). I'll escort you back to where you landed.

> **白厄：**走吧，丹恒，开拓者。我会带人护送两位回到你们坠落的地方。

**Phainon:** I know that a reunion in the future is wishful thinking, but at the very least, we can bid each other a proper farewell.

> **白厄：**我知道，重逢是种奢望。至少我们能让告别不那么草率。

Escorted by Phainon and the holy city guards, you and Dan Heng return to the crash site in the Abyss. Together, you salvage the Express' coach car.

With the assistance of an Okhema citizen resembling an Intellitron, you depart Amphoreus without suffering the so-called "curse from beyond the sky."

After returning to the Astral Express, you discover Black Swan has already left without telling anyone. March 7th's condition has gotten worse, but no one seems to know what the cause is.

Despite a fuel shortage, you complete a final warp jump, putting your hopes in the next location and how it might resolve all your worries.

As for the fate of Amphoreus, the Eternal Land, and the dramatic changes it will bring to the Cosmos... It has nothing to do with you, at least in your lifetime.

(Credits roll)

[Recap] Tribbie talked to Aglaea and learned that she had purposely given up your and Dan Heng's trust to help you build stronger connections with Phainon and Castorice, planning ahead for what might come.

**Tribbie:** ...Agy, this campaign is going to turn out fine, right?

> **阿格莱雅：**勿要犹疑，吾师。你是为黄金裔解读神谕之人，你言行中的踟蹰会令众人动摇。

> **阿格莱雅：**但我察觉到了遐蝶的不安。她信任他们，还私下联络了白厄，以为我不知情……

> **阿格莱雅：**所以将计就计，我索性将「流放」改成了「处刑」，用最极端的情境测量那两人的意志。

**Aglaea:** That's not important.

> **阿格莱雅：**你我终有一日要退出这舞台，翁法罗斯的命运将被移交至他人之手…那是你看到的未来，不是吗？

**Tribbie:** ...Yeah.

> **缇宝：**…嗯。

> **阿格莱雅：**那救世的神谕浑浊不清。它撺掇我们挑战众神，却从未告知预言中的「明天」是何模样。

**Aglaea:** Hence, we must be prepared. When our task is to craft the armor for the Deliverer, what harm is there if you and I don humble attire?

> **阿格莱雅：**所以，我们必须做好准备。你我身着褴褛又何妨？专心为「救世主」编织战衣便好。

**Tribbie:** Agy... Have you noticed...

Castrum Kremnos' Archaic Language Slate

...The black tide may be an indescribable enemy, but if exploited carefully, it can also become a sharp weapon pointed at our archenemy. Infiltrators can be instructed to transport a load of tainted Titankin covered in special earth, so that they may be smuggled among dromas caravan goods into Okhema. Upon entry, under the cover of the Curtain-Fall Hour, the personnel will stash the kin in various sections of the city. After a few days, the earth will weather and peel, suffocating the streets of Okhema with Titankin's tainted air. When this moment arrives, the Okhemans will be frantically searching for the source of the pollution. We just have to wait for the chaos to unfurl to take the final step to victory: Gather the heroic souls within the city, let the divine blade of wrath point at Okhema, and run Strife's blade through the entire city — after which, our forces will overwhelm them like an ocean wave crashing into them, and the long-awaited victory will finally fall into our grasp.

### Discuss a plan with Aglaea

> **白厄：**阿格莱雅——等等，缇宝老师在哪？

> **白厄：**城中的公民还和平日一样在寻欢作乐…你认为这算不上一次危机？

> **阿格莱雅：**若要以同样的方式应对每桩危机，奥赫玛会终日不得安宁。于无声中抹去威胁，让他们不必活在恐惧的阴霾之下，才是黄金裔的职责。

> **白厄：**天谴之锋已经瞄准了刻法勒的心脏。如果不设法阻止疯狂的泰坦，整座圣城都可能灰飞烟灭。

*(Quick Read)*

Five Great Virtues and Festival Notice

Festival Notice At the Action Hour in the Month of ███████, Day █, Kremnos Arena will hold a grand ceremony in commemoration of Nikador, and pray for Castrum Kremnos' prosperity, honing the battle souls of our folk, so that all may bask in the radiant glory of the Strife Titan's Five Great Virtues: Unfearing of blade at the throat, manifesting the visage of courage Unyielding to conniving treachery, protecting the crown of honor Unblinking of eyes burning bright, upholding the cornerstone of reason Unbending from wounds to flesh, forging characters of tenacity Undaunted of risking life to protect, embodying corpus of sacrifice. In the spirit of this gladiatorial festival, its warriors are all beacons of valor from all corners of Castrum Kremnos. The arena is in a wide open space — warriors can don sturdy armor and wield sharp lances, marching on a path of onslaught to finally face King Eurypon. Victors will seize fame, their names etched in glory, and the defeated will become fallen souls of heroes, unshaken in their pursuit of courage. The festival is a raucous affair, eagerly awaiting a surge of warriors who jostle to pledge themselves to call forth the divinity of the Titans. May Nikador defend Kremnos for eternity!

**Castorice:** Courage, Honor, Reason, Tenacity, Sacrifice...

> **格奈乌斯：**尼卡多利的灵魂已经腐烂，再无被救赎的可能。

- ▸ What can we do then?

**Gnaeus:** There is only one option.

> **格奈乌斯：**还有一个选择。

- ▸ How do we stop them then?

**Gnaeus:** There is only one way to do so.

> **格奈乌斯：**只有一条道路。

**Gnaeus:** The seal that binds their divinity is already broken. As long as we complete the soul-forging here, Nikador will be whole once more.

> **格奈乌斯：**束缚着神性的封印已经解除。只要在此完成铸魂，尼卡多利就能重归完整。

**Gnaeus:** You will go into battle with a Titan nearly at the zenith of their powers. And when that happens, the glory of slaying a god will also be presented before you.

> **格奈乌斯：**你们将与一位几近鼎盛的泰坦战斗；同时，弑神的荣光也将在你们面前绽放。

**Castorice:** ...What about you, Lord Gnaeus?

> **遐蝶：**…那你呢，格奈乌斯阁下？

**Gnaeus:** I will return to where I came from and become a part of Nikador's divinity once more.

> **格奈乌斯：**我将回归本源，成为尼卡多利神性的一个片段。

**Gnaeus:** In all likelihood, I will also be consumed by corruption. An inevitable course of action if we hope to end this madness.

> **格奈乌斯：**腐蚀大概会将我吞没吧？但为了终结疯狂，这一步无可避免。

**Castorice:** You are willing to... sacrifice yourself for a future you won't get to witness?

> **遐蝶：**你愿意为一个…自己无法见证的未来做出牺牲？

**Gnaeus:** This is my purpose, my origin. I am no mortal and I do not fear death... Even so, will you still weep for me?

> **格奈乌斯：**那是我的本源，我的使命。我并非凡人，也不惧怕死亡——即便如此，你也打算为我哀哭吗？

**Castorice:** All farewells in this world are sorrowful, Lord Gnaeus.

> **遐蝶：**这世上的离别皆是哀伤，阁下。

**Gnaeus:** While we haven't known each other for long, I have learned much from you. In return, I'll teach you a lesson of mine before I go...

> **格奈乌斯：**我们相识不久，但我已从你身上学到很多。那么，让我在临走前也教你一课吧——

**Gnaeus:** Do not detest your talent and do not abhor death. Journeys and epics are only glorious and magnificent because all things eventually fade to dust.

> **格奈乌斯：**不要嫌恶你的天赋，不要憎恨死亡。征途之所以伟大，史诗之所以壮阔，皆因万物终有逝去之时。

**Castorice:** ...

> **遐蝶：**……

**Castorice:** ...The embodiment of Strife telling me about the majesty of the world...? What an irony...

> **遐蝶：**…身为「纷争」的化身，却在向我讲述世界的壮美吗？真是讽刺……

**Gnaeus:** Civilizations only grow under strife. Humans instinctively loathe suffering, yet suffering is the only thing that teaches them to stand unyielding and tall...

> **格奈乌斯：**只有被置于纷争之下，文明方能成长。人生来便憎恶苦难，但唯有苦难能教人屹立……

- ▸ We can't put this off any longer.
- ▸ I will do as you wish.
- ▸ If this is meant to be... then I will fight you.

**Gnaeus:** He/She knows what it truly means to go to battle. Perhaps he/she can be your inspiration.

> **格奈乌斯：**他/她比你更懂得战斗的意义，也许能成为你的启迪。

**Gnaeus:** If you die by my lance, then there's no need to reflect on what I said. But if you should send me to my grave... promise me that you will use my Coreflame to fuel a roaring blaze and continue the fight against the black tide.

> **格奈乌斯：**你若死在我的矛下，便无需再试着理解这些话语；你若能将我埋葬…答应我，你们会将火种当作柴薪，升起烈焰，继续与黑潮抗争。

**Castorice:** On behalf of all the Chrysos Heirs, I... promise you.

> **遐蝶：**我…代表全体黄金裔，答应你的请求。

**Gnaeus:** I am Nikador, the Lance of Fury, the messenger of turmoil, and the embodiment of Strife!

> **格奈乌斯：**我是天谴之矛，尼卡多利——乱世的使者，纷争的化身！

**Gnaeus:** Remember this: I am the scar that this world needs!

> **格奈乌斯：**记住——我是这世间必要的伤痕！

> **白厄：**不用再挥剑了……

> **遐蝶：**「天谴之矛」已然不再，黄金裔距离神谕中的创世更近了一步。

> **遐蝶：**格奈乌斯阁下……

> **遐蝶：**请告诉我…你是否知道塞纳托斯身在何方？

Nikador's Corpse: ...

> **遐蝶：**我的远行还要继续，哪怕要走遍翁法罗斯的每个角落……

Nikador's Corpse: (Titan's fragmented voice)

**Castorice:** I understand... The Chrysos Heirs will fulfill the promise we made and see your unfinished work to its end.

> **遐蝶：**我明白…黄金裔一定会尊重约定，延续你未竟之事。

> **遐蝶：**在诗人的笔下，你仍会是疯狂和堕落的化身，燃尽了荣光的战神。但此时此刻…我愿意送你前往开满花朵的温柔乡。

> **遐蝶：**永别了，泰坦。

[Recap] Phainon reunited with Aglaea and Tribbie, carrying the Coreflame. Aglaea hoped Phainon would take on the mantle of Strife. Phainon was willing to make that sacrifice, but believed Mydei was the one truly fit for it.

> **白厄：**我们完成使命了，缇宝老师——没有缺肢断腿，只是掌心多出了一颗滚烫的火种。

**Aglaea:** I can feel the Coreflame's warmth... Well done, Phainon.

> **阿格莱雅：**我能感应到火种的温度…做得好，白厄。

> **阿格莱雅：**翁法罗斯已经许久未见一位新的半神诞生了。若你能填补尼卡多利留下的空缺，对于圣城的公民将会是极大的鼓舞。

> **白厄：**我…必须向你们坦白，我认为万敌是更合适的人选。他曾是悬锋城的王储，生命中的大部分时间都在随尼卡多利一同征战。

> **白厄：**他比我更称得上「纷争」这个词的重量。

> **阿格莱雅：**你所说种种，也许正是迈德漠斯不愿接过这颗火种的理由。

> **阿格莱雅：**我不怀疑他的力量和资质…成神的景象，他也一定在脑海中预演过千遍。但他之所以推托，正是不想再带领族人走上相同的道路。

**Aglaea:** So I will ask you once more... Have you made up your mind, Phainon of Aedes Elysiae?

> **阿格莱雅：**所以…你做好觉悟了吗，哀丽秘榭的白厄？

**Aglaea:** Very good.

> **阿格莱雅：**很好。

**Tribbie:** This day has finally come, Snowy. You've been wanting this all along, right?

> **缇宝：**终于等到这一天了，小白。这是你一直以来的愿望，对吧？

> **白厄：**回到当年，假如神谕没有找到我…我肯定还在漫无目的地流浪。此时此刻，我存在的意义就是为黄金裔的使命奉献一切。

**Aglaea:** I never once doubted your resolve.

> **阿格莱雅：**我从未怀疑过你的决心。

**Aglaea:** This time tomorrow, the Chrysos Heirs will gather at the Vortex of Genesis to surrender the Coreflame that once belonged to Nikador, the Lance of Fury.

> **阿格莱雅：**明日同一时分，黄金裔将在创世涡心集合，归还天谴之矛，尼卡多利的火种。

> **阿格莱雅：**记住，这不是祭司们亘古不变的仪式。无人知晓凡人需要付出多大的代价，才能接下「纷争」的神权，你的前路仍充满变数。

[Recap] You and Dan Heng reflected on the day's events in the baths. Phainon arrived to invite you both to the Coreflame ritual tomorrow at the Vortex of Genesis, to return the Strife Coreflame. As a trusted comrade, he hoped your presence would give him some strength.

> **第四面镜：**原来如此。那黑塔女士，经过你的专业判断——是哪个扇区出故障了？

> **黑塔：**简单，当然是三个全都出问题了。

**Fourth Mirror:** ...

> **第四面镜：**……

**Herta:** No sweat. They'll be up and running again after some simple technical tweaking.

> **黑塔：**没什么大不了的，只需要一点小小的技术性调整。

**Herta:** The three zones are compartmentalized, so we're going to have to troubleshoot them one at a time. Let's start with the Energy Supply Zone. Get ready, and then we'll go over.

> **黑塔：**三个扇区是独立分开的，所以排查问题也得一个一个来。你要是准备好了，我们就先去「能源区」检查下情况。

**Fourth Mirror:** I'm just a mirror, Madam Herta! If you're ready, then your reflection in me will also be ready to go.

> **第四面镜：**我可是面镜子，黑塔女士！只要镜子里的您准备好了，那我就准备好了。

**Fourth Mirror:** Brr... Why is this Energy Supply Zone... so cold? Brr... I—I'm already frosting up!

> **第四面镜：**咯咯咯…这「能源区」…怎么这么冷啊？咯咯咯…我的镜面，都快要结霜了！

**Herta:** Hmph, why else? I've converted the energy core that supplies all of Herta Space Station into Imaginary state and stored it in this zone. If not for the ultra-low temperature here, the space station would have long blasted off into the sky.

> **黑塔：**你以为呢？整个黑塔空间站的能源都被我转化成了虚数态，蓄存在这个扇区。要是不配置点超低温环境，空间站早就被炸上天了。

**Fourth Mirror:** Brrr... But... aren't we already... in the sky?

> **第四面镜：**咯咯咯…但是…我们本来不就…在天上吗？

**Herta:** ...You're just a mirror. I don't need you to take on comic relief too.

> **黑塔：**…作为一面镜子，你的幽默感是不是有点过剩了？

**Herta:** Where did those things come from? Corrupted data? Reanimated systemic rust? No, not likely... It can't be the Legion's second ambush, right...?

> **黑塔：**那些家伙是哪来的？坏死数据？活性化的系统锈斑？不应该啊…总不能是军团又打过来了吧？

**Herta:** Meh, whatever. We have to get rid of them anyway. Alright, stay close and try to avoid shattering to pieces!

> **黑塔：**不管了，反正都得清理干净。跟紧我，可别轻易碎了！

### Clear the abnormalities in the Energy Supply Zone

> **第四面镜：**女士，这是什么？

> **黑塔：**那个啊，应该就是个普通的忆泡吧？没什么特别的，其它舱段里也到处都是。

**Fourth Mirror:** Hmm... is that so.

### Go to Computing Zone

*(After defeating the enemies)*

**Fourth Mirror:** Brrr... Aaaaand Madam Herta never fails to impress! Those things are no match for you!

**Herta:** Hmph, a piece of cake!

**Herta:** Now that this place is cleaned up, let's set everything in order and move on to the next zone.

*(Interact with the Audience System terminal)*

**Fourth Mirror:** Wh—What in the...!? How did all these disgusting bugs get in here?

> **第四面镜：**这…这是什么？！哪来这么多恶心虫子？

**Herta:** We're going to need out-of-this-world computing capability if we hope to meet Droidhead. If we can't churn out calculations faster than a yottaFLOPS computer, then we can forget about having THEM notice our existence.

> **黑塔：**要想见到机器头，算力至少得匹配上。每秒执行的浮点运算达不到个几万垓次，祂压根就感受不到你的存在。

**Herta:** But computers with too high a computing capability tend to attract Computing Stings like them. No one knows how these creatures evolved from their progenitor — the Propagation.

> **黑塔：**但算力太高也有坏处，就是会引来这群「算力蜇虫」——「繁育」子嗣中的一支，也不知道是怎么进化出来的。

**Fourth Mirror:** Ah, the mysteries of life. Hah! But even so, they can't compare to a talking mirror~

> **第四面镜：**生命，很神奇吧？不过还是比不上一面会说话的镜子，哈。

**Fourth Mirror:** A question on the side, Madam Herta: You have these powerful supercomputers and all, so... um, what are they normally programmed to calculate?

> **第四面镜：**顺带一提，黑塔女士——你既然有算力如此强大的超级计算机，那它平时到底在…呃…算些什么？

**Herta:** Is that important? All that matters is that they work and are able to attract Droidhead's attention.

> **黑塔：**这重要吗？我只需要让它运转起来，能引起机器头的注意就行了。

**Fourth Mirror:** *sigh* ...I suggest meeting Miss Asta for a chat when you have time.

> **第四面镜：**唉…有空的话，您还是跟艾丝妲小姐聊聊吧。

### Clear the abnormalities in the Computing Zone

> *Inside the Herta Space Station, Herta's audience with Nous had yet to end. Upon investigation, all three sectors within the terminal are malfunctioning, requiring Herta to personally attend to each one to resolve the issues.*

*(Investigate the memory bubble, optional)*

> **第四面镜：**这里也有个忆泡……

**Herta:** Why are you so interested in it?

> **黑塔：**那么关心它干嘛？

**Fourth Mirror:** Okay, I'll admit it. I find them somewhat... creepy?

> **第四面镜：**好吧，我就是觉得有点…诡异？

*(After defeating the enemies)*

**Fourth Mirror:** Ew, yuck! Help me! Goo from those bugs got onto my reflective surface!

> **第四面镜：**救命！虫子的体液，崩到我的镜面上了！

**Herta:** Clean up after yourself. Can't you just wipe it off with your hands?

> **黑塔：**自己的事情自己做，用手擦掉不就行了？

**Fourth Mirror:** ...Ahaha! Good one there...

> **第四面镜：**哈、哈！您真幽默……

**Herta:** With those annoying bugs squashed, we're done fixing the Computing Zone too.

> **黑塔：**解决了烦人的虫子，「算力区」也算打扫干净了。

**Herta:** These computers will get up to speed soon. Now we're just one final step away from requesting an audience with Droidhead.

> **黑塔：**计算机很快就能恢复运转，距离谒见机器头就只差最后一步。

**Fourth Mirror:** Our last stop is the... Communications Zone?

> **第四面镜：**最后一个扇区…是叫「通信区」来着？

**Herta:** Oh? You have a good memory. Let's go see what's wrong in that zone.

> **黑塔：**你记得还挺清楚嘛。走吧，去看看那边出了什么状况。

### Go to the Communications Zone

*(Interact with the Audience System terminal)*

**Fourth Mirror:** Wh—What's all this? I didn't think your puppets would be in here too, Madam Herta.

> **第四面镜：**这…这是？黑塔女士，没想到连这里都有你的人偶。

**Herta:** Unsurprisingly, the Communications Zone is where a bridge for communication will be formed between Droidhead and the space station.

> **黑塔：**「通信区」，顾名思义，就是负责建立空间站与机器头之间沟通桥梁的扇区。

**Herta:** Aggregating the Path Energy of the Erudition, then firing a "bond" at the Aeon. This is as good as tapping someone on the shoulder, who's sure to turn back to look at you.

> **黑塔：**将「智识」的命途能量集中于一处，然后向星神发射一条「纽带」——等于从背后拍了拍某人的肩膀，他的反应肯定是回头看你一眼。

**Herta:** Alas, our work is never this simple. Putting aside all that onerous legwork, a single glance from Droidhead... could vaporize the entire space station if we're ill-prepared.

> **黑塔：**但这项工作可不简单，且不说前置准备有多麻烦，机器头的一瞥…稍有不慎就可能毁掉整个空间站。

**Fourth Mirror:** I see... So letting the space station go into hibernation is a protective measure of sorts? To prevent it from slipping into operational overload and turning into a scrap world?

> **第四面镜：**噢…所以让空间站进入静默是一种保护手段，避免让设备运转过载甚至报废？

**Herta:** Hm, very perceptive for a mirror.

> **黑塔：**悟性挺高的嘛，镜子。

**Fourth Mirror:** Mirror see (and hear), mirror do! Though I neither have eyes nor ears, haha!

> **第四面镜：**耳濡目染，耳濡目染——虽然我既没耳朵也没眼睛，哈！

**Fourth Mirror:** This zone... seems to be functioning fine? These puppets are doing their job, and I don't see any obvious malfunction...

> **第四面镜：**这片扇区…好像还挺正常的？这些人偶也都在运作，没看出有什么故障……

**Herta:** Things may look fine only on the surface.

> **黑塔：**这可不好说啊。

**Fourth Mirror:** Madam Herta, behind you—

> **第四面镜：**黑塔女士，您身后——

**Fourth Mirror:** ...Hm? What does she want?

> **第四面镜：**…嗯？她想干嘛？

**Herta:** Let me take a look. This puppet... should be #2757.

> **黑塔：**让我看看，这个人偶…应该是#2757。

**Fourth Mirror:** H—How did you tell!?

> **第四面镜：**这、这你是怎么认出来的？！

**Herta:** Shh! Don't distract me when I'm checking the puppet's parameters. #2757...

> **黑塔：**嘘！别打岔，我在检查人偶的状态。#2757……

**Herta:** Hmm... That's strange...

> **黑塔：**嗯…奇怪了……

**Fourth Mirror:** Er... What's wrong, Madam Herta?

> **第四面镜：**呃…怎么了，黑塔女士？

**Herta:** The "Replica Personality Module"... is off. That can't be. This parameter is set to be enabled by default.

> **黑塔：**「人格映射模块」…已关闭。奇怪，这项参数应该是默认打开的才对吧？

**Herta:** And the "Utterly Absurd Module"... has also been disabled? Something's definitely wrong here. I checked a couple days ago to make sure every puppet has it switched on...

> **黑塔：**「蛮不讲理模块」…也关闭了？这个更不合理，我前几天才检查过一遍，确保所有人偶都开着它……

**Herta:** The "Attack Anything Everything Module"...

> **黑塔：**「无差别攻击模块」……

**Fourth Mirror:** No, wait. Why do you even have such presets!?

> **第四面镜：**不是，为什么还有这种预设啊？！

**Herta:** ...is enabled.

> **黑塔：**…已打开。

**Herta Puppet:** Intruder. Detected.

> **黑塔人偶：**发现。入侵。

**Herta:** Drats. Come and help me, mirror...

> **黑塔：**这下坏了——镜子，帮我！

**Herta Puppet:** Anything, everything. Attack!

> **黑塔人偶：**无差别。攻击！

*(Begin battle against Memory Zone Meme "Heartbreaker" ×3, Memory Zone Meme "Allseer" ×2)*

**Herta:** These are... memetic remnants of the Remembrance?

> **黑塔：**这些家伙…是「记忆」的模因残渣吧？

**Herta:** So they were the ones that corrupted and tampered with the settings of my puppets?

> **黑塔：**就是它们污染、篡改了人偶的设置吗？

*(After the battle)*

**Fourth Mirror:** Egad, what a close shave... But why are these Remembrance memes even showing up here?

> **第四面镜：**哎呀，可真是千钧一发…所以说，为什么这里会出现「记忆」的迷因？

**Herta:** That's not the only one. Look at them. #7631 and #10136... are affected too.

> **黑塔：**可不止这一个。看那边——#7631，#10136…都是一样的问题。

**Fourth Mirror:** Can't you do a remote factory reset on them?

> **第四面镜：**就不能远程把它们的参数调回来吗？

**Herta:** Sorry to disappoint. The "Absolutely Not Module" has been enabled, so not even I get to bypass its permissions. Whoopsie.

> **黑塔：**很遗憾，不能。因为「拒绝配合模块」已经被勾选上了——连我也绕不过它的权限，嘻嘻。

**Fourth Mirror:** ...What were you really trying to achieve with these creations?

> **第四面镜：**…您到底是出于什么心理设计的这些玩意？

**Herta:** Just some toys to amuse myself with.

> **黑塔：**当时就是设计着玩而已。

**Herta:** Finding the source of the corruption can wait. These puppets no longer have use now that I'm here. Take them all out! Defective goods ought to be sent back to the repair center!

> **黑塔：**寻找污染源头的事可以晚点再说。既然我本人已经到场，也就不需要这些人偶了。来吧，把残次品统统送进维修厂！

**Fourth Mirror:** I... urge you to go easy on them, for the sake of the space station...

> **第四面镜：**为了空间站考虑，我建议您下手别太重……

### Defeat the out-of-control Herta puppets

### Head deeper to investigate the puppet's signal

*(After defeating the enemies)*

**Fourth Mirror:** They're completely battered, save for... their heads?

> **第四面镜：**虽然已经彻底报废了，不过脑袋倒还完整？

**Herta:** Never strike someone in the face — the same goes for puppets.

> **黑塔：**打人不打脸，对人偶也是一个道理~

**Herta:** I'm picking up signals from more puppets somewhere farther in. Let's go take a look.

> **黑塔：**更深处好像还有人偶的信号，咱们去看看。

*(Investigate the memory bubble, optional)*

**Fourth Mirror:** A Memory Bubble tucked away this far in?

> **第四面镜：**这么深入的地方，居然也有忆泡？

**Herta:** Heh... I think I know what this is about now. This is interesting.

> **黑塔：**嘻…我有些头绪了，有趣。

**Herta:** But we can leave that for later... Let's take care of the malfunctioning puppets first.

> **黑塔：**谜底可以留到晚点再揭晓…先解决有故障的人偶吧？

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Herta:** Ah, #0988. The pride of my creations!

> **黑塔：**啊，#0988——我的得意之作！

**Fourth Mirror:** ...Should I even be curious...?

> **第四面镜：**…我都不知道该不该问了。

**Herta:** Puppets before her were all as dull as brick walls, mundane and uninspired. I distinctly recall #0988 being the first puppet I imbued with... a magical surprise. Some other puppets that came later also got to enjoy similar privileges.

> **黑塔：**在它之前的人偶全都一模一样，千篇一律，无聊得很。我记得很清楚，从#0988开始，我会随机往人偶里塞一些小惊喜……

**Fourth Mirror:** ...Good to know, but did anyone ask!?

> **第四面镜：**虽然但是，谁问您了？

**Herta:** Fear not, mirror. You have to remember that being a genius means you're destined to solve challenge after challenge. There's no time for rest.

> **黑塔：**别怕，镜子——你要记住，天才的宿命，就是要一刻不停地解开出现在眼前的难题。

**Fourth Mirror:** ...C—Can I just be on the sidelines this time? Pretty please!?

> **第四面镜：**…我申请旁边观战，行吗？！

*(Begin battle against Herta ErrorModel ×20, which transforms into Swarm: True Sting ×20)*

*(After the battle)*

**Fourth Mirror:** Phew, that was close... I almost shattered...

> **第四面镜：**呼，好险…差点碎了一地……

**Herta:** Don't be melodramatic. Working even a single brain cell of mine to take out foes like her is an outright waste of my smarts.

> **黑塔：**别夸大其词，应付这种对手，让我动一个脑细胞都算是高看它了。

**Fourth Mirror:** I take it we've taken care of the problems in all three zones? Does that mean...?

> **第四面镜：**所以，现在三个扇区的麻烦都解决了？这是不是就表示……

**Herta:** Not yet. There's something else we need to deal with.

> **黑塔：**在那之前，咱们还得先解决一件小事。

**Herta:** It seems we've had a little tagalong all this while...

> **黑塔：**从刚才开始，我们身后好像就一直跟着条小尾巴呢……

**Fourth Mirror:** You've got quite the sharp eye, Madam Herta. Would you like us to help?

> **第四面镜：**您的观察真是细致入微，黑塔女士。需要我们帮忙吗？

**Herta:** Sure, go ahead and summon the whole team, Fourth Mirror.

> **黑塔：**好啊，把大家都叫出来吧，第四面镜——

**Herta:** Considering the gravity of our endeavor, we can't allow a little thief to spoil the show.

> **黑塔：**我们要做的事如此重要，可不能被一个小小的窃贼给钻了空子啊。

**Herta:** Hmph, parasite of the Remembrance. Do you truly think your atrocious disguise can outwit the keen sight of a genius like myself?

> **黑塔：**「记忆」的寄生虫，你该不会真的以为，这套拙劣的伪装能骗过天才的眼睛吧？

**Herta:** Once you're reflected in my mirrors...

> **黑塔：**一旦被我的镜子照到……

**Herta:** ...you can forget about escaping.

> **黑塔：**…就别想再逃跑咯。

**Memosnatcher:** Oh dear, oh dear. This is bad...

> **窃忆者：**糟了，这下糟了……

**Memosnatcher:** Right, stay alive and again I can strive... Retreat into the Memory Zone for now, I can always regroup and try again!

> **窃忆者：**留得青山在，不愁没柴烧…先逃回忆域，以后的事再说！

**Herta:** Someone hasn't been paying attention.

> **黑塔：**哎，看来有些人没有用心听呢。

**Herta:** Didn't hear me, did you? With those petty tricks of yours... do you actually think you can slip away right under my nose?

> **黑塔：**我已经说过了吧？就凭你这点本事…也想逃出我的掌心？

**Memosnatcher:** Herta... Madam Herta, wait! Wh—What do you want with me...?

> **窃忆者：**黑塔…黑塔大人！你、你想做什么……

**Memosnatcher:** I... I'll spill it all! The Garden's secrets and what they're after... everything! P—Please just spare me...

> **窃忆者：**我…我全都告诉你！忆庭的秘密，他们在觊觎的东西…我全都愿意说出来！请饶、饶了我吧……

**Herta:** Well, is that all you've got to say while your life is at stake? That's hardly worth anything.

> **黑塔：**哎呀，这么紧急的关头，你能想到的救命说辞就只有这么几句？这可不行。

**Herta:** Need a little refresher? I am Herta, Genius Society #83, the one to unravel the mystery behind the imaginary leakage phenomenon, and keyholder of the Imaginary Implosion Pulse... Do you honestly believe I'd seek any counsel from you?

> **黑塔：**需要再提醒你一下吗？我是天才俱乐部#83黑塔，解开虚数流溢之谜的学者，虚数坍缩武器的执钥人…你觉得，我需要从你身上获得任何答案吗？

**Memosnatcher:** I... I...

> **窃忆者：**我…我……

**Herta:** Hush now, keep quiet. This is my moment to shine, so just listen.

> **黑塔：**嘘，别出声。这可是我的高光时刻，你只要乖乖听着就行了。

**Herta:** You crept in here to steal Nous' memories, didn't you?

> **黑塔：**你潜入这里，无非就是为了窃取博识尊的记忆，不是么？

**Herta:** And messing with my precious Audience System is just a ruse to lure me here so I can lead you to THEM...

> **黑塔：**把我宝贵的「谒见系统」搞得一团糟，也是为了把我本人引来这里，为你们带路……

**Herta:** What a shame. You're only a step away from completing the mission assigned to you...

> **黑塔：**真可惜，你只差一点就能完成任务，回去交差了……

**Memosnatcher:** Mmph...! Nngh...!

> **窃忆者：**唔…唔……

**Herta:** ...As if. Truth is, you never stood a chance from the get-go, teehee!

> **黑塔：**…开玩笑的，你从头到尾都没有一丝机会，嘻嘻。

**Herta:** Think I only have Fourth Mirror following me around? Then you've let your guard down, because her siblings have long been watching your every move. Now, let me think... What kind of punishment should I subject a brazen thief like you to?

> **黑塔：**别看只有第四面镜跟在我身边，它的兄弟姐妹们早就把你的行踪一览无余了。让我想想…该怎么惩罚一个胆大包天的窃贼呢？

**Herta:** Ooh, I know! You Garden folk have a fondness for mirrors, huh... How about letting you enjoy some quality time with your favorite object — a mirror?

> **黑塔：**噢，我有主意了！忆庭的人都很喜欢镜子，对吧？那我干脆就成人之美，让你多跟镜子做做伴吧？

**Herta:** As for the length of your punishment... Let's keep it mildly punitive. How about a hundredth of a lifetime? That should be enough for some serious self-reflection.

> **黑塔：**惩罚的时长…也不该太严苛，就取百分之一的人生吧？这点时间，足够你好好反省了。

**Herta:** Aww, cheer up. You can still do your part for this world while trapped in a mirror. For all you know, your weak Path Energy could just help me get Droidhead looking this way.

> **黑塔：**别怕，就算被藏进了镜子里，你还是能为这个世界做出贡献的。你身上那点微薄的命途能量，说不定就能帮我引来机器头的注意呢？

**Herta:** Ah, I should make myself clear. When I said a hundredth of a lifetime...

> **黑塔：**啊，忘记说了。刚刚提到的百分之一……

**Herta:** ...I meant my lifetime.

> **黑塔：**…乘以的是我的寿命哦。

**Herta:** Ahh, at long last... We can finally be at ease now that we've nipped those problems in the bud.

> **黑塔：**哈…一身轻松，总算把问题连根拔起了。

**Fourth Mirror:** Omnomnom... Mmm, this is yummy...

> **第四面镜：**吧唧吧唧…好吃，好吃……

**Herta:** Cut that out! Those weird noises make me seem like I've got a few screws loose. Put that little thief to sleep for now. We can decide her fate later.

> **黑塔：**别发出奇怪的动静啊，显得我精神不太正常似的。让那小贼先好好睡一觉吧，晚点再决定该怎么处置她。

**Fourth Mirror:** Oh, sweet and venerable Madam Herta, when did your heart turn this tender?

> **第四面镜：**哎呀，可爱又可敬的黑塔女士，您的心肠什么时候变得这么软了？

> **黑塔：**「谒见系统」已经重启。总算到了这个时候……

> **命途之声•二：** 0002……

> **黑塔：**你是在耍我吗……

### Approach the Second Mirror to advance the audience's progress

> **第二面镜：**黑塔女士！那些声音，又出现了！

> **第二面镜：**怎么办，我们该怎么办？！

### Approach the Third Mirror to advance the audience's progress

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Third Mirror:** Wanna listen in on them? Hyuk hyuk hyuk...

> **第三面镜：**您要不要也来听听？嘻嘻嘻……

**Herta:** Third Mirror! Stop that nightmarish cackle. It's giving me goosebumps.

> **黑塔：**第三面镜，你能不能收一收那让人起鸡皮疙瘩的笑声？

> **黑塔：**你刚才提到了「外面的家伙」？难道是……

**Third Mirror:** Eheheh... Why don't you listen for yourself?

> **第三面镜：**欸嘿嘿…女士，要不您自己听？

> **命途之声•一：**她昏倒了？

**Voice of Path 2:** Mm-hmm, looks like it.

> **命途之声•二：**嗯，看来如此。

**Herta:** ...

> **黑塔：**……

**Herta:** ...This isn't right. I'm sure we've got intruders in the lab!

> **黑塔：**…不对劲，肯定是有人闯进实验室了！

**Voice of Path 2:** Should we rouse her, Mr. Yang?

> **命途之声•二：**要叫醒她么，瓦尔特先生？

**Voice of Path 1:** ...Let's do it. I'm not sure she'll be happy about it, but we have no time to waste.

> **命途之声•一：**嗯，就这么做吧。虽然有些无礼，但我们的确在赶时间。

**Herta:** ...Wait! S—Stop whatever you're about to do...

> **黑塔：**…等等！等等等等……

> **黑塔：**不行，不行，绝对不行！不管你们是谁，别在这个时候打断我！

**Herta:** I must continue forward... Just a few more steps and I'll be able to see Droidhead!

> **黑塔：**我得继续前进…几步，只差几步我就能见到机器头了！

### Approach the Fourth Mirror to advance the audience's progress

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Herta:** ...

> **黑塔：**……

**Fourth Mirror:** Is something wrong, Madam Herta?

> **第四面镜：**出什么问题了，黑塔女士？

**Herta:** No, it's nothing. I was just thinking...

> **黑塔：**不，没什么。我只是在想……

**Fourth Mirror:** Um, Madam Herta... I think you'll want to listen to the voices from beyond this space...

> **第四面镜：**呃，黑塔女士…从狭间外面传进来的声音，您最好听听……

**Voice of Path 1:** I'll leave it to you then, Mr. Sunday.

> **命途之声•一：**能麻烦你吗，星期日先生？

> **命途之声•二：**交给我吧。

> **黑塔：**等等…他们想干嘛？

> **命途之声•二：**失礼了，黑塔女士——

**Herta:** Is this... the Harmony?

> **黑塔：**这是…「同谐」？

**Herta:** Ridiculous! Hoping to stop me with such cheap parlor tricks? Not a chance!

> **黑塔：**无理取闹，想靠这种小伎俩影响我？门都没有！

**Voice of Path 2:** She easily dispelled the Resonance with her subconscious mind... as expected of an Emanator.

> **命途之声•二：**她靠着潜意识就轻易化解了「同调」…不愧是令使。

**Voice of Path 1:** Is there something else we can try?

> **命途之声•一：**还有别的办法吗？

**Fourth Mirror:** Bahaha! Presumptuous fellows. Who are you to interrupt Madam Herta's experiment?

> **第四面镜：**哈哈！不自量力的家伙，还以为自己有能力打断黑塔女士的实验？

**Fourth Mirror:** Let's ignore them and keep going. We haven't seen Nous yet!

> **第四面镜：**别理他们，我们接着去见博识尊吧！

**Herta:** ...

> **黑塔：**……

> **黑塔：**…还是算了，镜子，我们先离开这里吧。

**Fourth Mirror:** Leave? S—Surely you're not suggesting... we stop the Audience Experiment right now?

> **第四面镜：**出去？您、您的意思是…要在这个时候中断谒见实验？

**Fourth Mirror:** That's beyond all reason, ma'am! We went through so much just to get here! Now we're just one step away from making contact with the Aeon THEMSELF... Please, you know how insanely costly it is to reboot the Audience System!

> **第四面镜：**这太疯狂了，女士！我们费了那么大力气才来到这里，只差一步就能接触到星神本尊了…重启一次「谒见系统」的成本有多高，您也知道！

**Herta:** But if I were to ignore those two intruders and continue onward... When Droidhead casts THEIR gaze upon the space station, the blast of energy generated in that instant is sure to burn them to a crisp.

> **黑塔：**但要是我不管那两个入侵者，继续往前…等机器头向空间站投下目光，那一瞬间的能量保准会把他们烧成焦炭。

**Herta:** Experiments can always be repeated, but humans cannot be brought back to life.

> **黑塔：**实验还有机会做第二次，人没了可就再没办法复活了。

**Fourth Mirror:** ...

> **第四面镜：**……

**Fourth Mirror:** *sob* Oh Madam Herta, so selfless and kind! I'm a truly lucky mirror to have found the best possible master...

> **第四面镜：**呜呜…博爱又温柔的黑塔女士！本镜可真是跟对了主人啊……

**Herta:** Enough. Don't use that nauseatingly fawning tone on me!

> **黑塔：**给我停下，不许用那么恶心的腔调说话！

**Herta:** ...Looks like I'll have to abort this prematurely again.

> **黑塔：**哎…看来这次也要半途而废了。

**Herta:** But mark my words, Droidhead: I will be back for you...

> **黑塔：**机器头——我会回来的，你给我等着……

**Welt:** ...Madam Herta? You're finally awake.

> **瓦尔特：**…黑塔女士？您醒了？

**Herta:** ...You two...

> **黑塔：**哎…你们两个……

**Herta:** ...

> **黑塔：**……

**Herta:** My plan is ruined! And it's all thanks to you!

> **黑塔：**我的计划，全毁在你们手上了！

**Sunday:** ...Your plan?

> **星期日：**…您的计划？

**Welt:** My sincerest apologies, Madam Herta... We weren't aware this was a part of your plan.

> **瓦尔特：**很抱歉，黑塔女士…我们并不知道您另有安排。

**Welt:** The space station was in complete darkness when we arrived, so Sunday and I assumed something bad had happened. We continued further into the space station, hoping to find the cause of the blackout... and that's when we chanced upon your... actual self.

> **瓦尔特：**我们抵达空间站时，只发现这里漆黑一片，我和星期日都以为出现了什么紧急情况。我们一路下行寻找事故根源，没想到会在此处碰到您…的正身。

**Herta:** How did you get in?

> **黑塔：**你们…是怎么进来的？

**Sunday:** The door wasn't locked.

> **星期日：**那道门没锁。

**Herta:** ...

> **黑塔：**……

**Herta:** Alright, fine... I am a person of reason, after all. You're not to blame for this.

> **黑塔：**好吧，好吧…怎么说我也是个讲道理的人，这件事是怪不到你们身上。

**Herta:** I am familiar with Mr. Yang of the Astral Express, but the one next to you...

> **黑塔：**星穹列车的杨先生我有印象，你旁边这位是……

**Welt:** ...Looks like Madam Herta doesn't pay much attention to cosmic news.

> **瓦尔特：**…看来黑塔女士对银河间的新闻不太敏感。

**Sunday:** Please pardon my manners. It is unbecoming of me to forget the most basic of courtesies amid all that has happened... My name's Sunday, and I'm just a passenger of the Express.

> **星期日：**请原谅，情急之下我竟忘记了最基本的礼仪——我叫星期日，只是一个普通的搭车客。

**Herta:** And this unremarkable passenger... happens to be a Harmony Pathstrider? Hmph, do you take me for a child?

> **黑塔：**一个默默无闻的旅人…却是「同谐」的命途行者？哼，谁信啊。

**Herta:** In any case, I don't suppose you came all the way here just to check on how the space station is doing?

> **黑塔：**所以，我猜两位大驾光临，肯定不是为了观光吧？

**Welt:** Well, we were worried for a moment there... but it's good to know the space station isn't in any kind of trouble.

> **瓦尔特：**虽然眼下的情境与我们设想中大有出入…但无论如何，空间站没事就好。

**Welt:** We're here because of Amphoreus, Madam Herta.

> **瓦尔特：**黑塔女士，我们是为「翁法罗斯」而来。

**Herta:** Hm. Why does that not surprise me?

> **黑塔：**哦，一点都不意外。

**Welt:** Oh? Something occured to you?

> **瓦尔特：**您想到什么了吗？

**Herta:** No, I've told Himeko everything I know. Nothing about the world sounds right. Droidhead has cast THEIR gaze upon numerous worlds in the past... and every one of them is etched in my mind. So how could I possibly not know about it?

> **黑塔：**没有，我都和姬子说了，这事怪得很。机器头曾经向那么多世界投去过目光…它们全都刻在我的脑子里，怎么可能有漏网之鱼？

**Herta:** Perhaps those Garden folks lied to you. Anyhow, I've looked up the name Amphoreus in the Simulated Universe. Though clearly irrelevant results have already been filtered out...

> **黑塔：**要么就是你们被忆庭的人忽悠了。总之，我先用模拟宇宙检索了这四个字，虽然已经筛选掉了一眼无关的结果……

**Herta:** There's still an unbelievably long list of generated results. Something about how the Divine Amphoreus team will swap out their current crest emblem for one in King Baviru's likeness in the next season. I hope that can be of some help to you.

> **黑塔：**但依旧剩下一份天文数字的清单——什么「『翁法罗斯神王队』下一赛季将使用王者巴维鲁取代队徽」——希望能帮上各位的忙吧。

**Welt:** ...We may as well take a look at it.

> **瓦尔特：**…无妨，请给我们看看吧。

**Herta:** I still don't get it, though. Didn't the Astral Express already depart some time ago? Is that world so incredibly spectacular that it warrants this return trip?

> **黑塔：**不过我还是好奇，星穹列车不是早就出发了么？那世界真有这么神奇，至于让你们特地折返一趟？

**Welt:** Well, that's because... we're caught between a rock and a hard place.

> **瓦尔特：**因为…我们陷入了进退两难的境地。

**Welt:** If Amphoreus is indeed inextricably entwined with Aeons, Paths, and even the Erudition, we're going to need the wisdom of a genius to help us. Especially now that we've seen the planet for ourselves.

> **瓦尔特：**尤其是在亲眼见证后，如果翁法罗斯真的与星神、命途，乃至「智识」密切相关，我们需要天才的智慧。

**Herta:** Elaborate a little on this "rock and a hard place" you're caught between?

> **黑塔：**你口中的「进退两难」——要不展开说说吧，杨先生？

**Welt:** We've lost all contact with two indispensable companions since they entered Amphoreus.

> **瓦尔特：**对我们而言十分重要的两位同伴，在进入翁法罗斯后便杳无音讯。

**Welt:** And that's not all... One of our other companions is suffering from a serious and inexplicable affliction.

> **瓦尔特：**此外…还有另一位同伴，身上出现了我们难以解释的可怕症状。

Returning to the Trailblazer's POV...

When you have a chance to make a choice, make one that you know you won't regret.

## 104　Hero, Bear Thy Coreflame　英雄啊，且握住那枚火种

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域："Eternal Holy City" Okhema　|　角色：Trailblazer; Himeko; March 7th; Pom-Pom; Castorice; Phainon; Dan Heng; Aglaea; Tribbie; Trinnon; Cyrene; Mydei; Mem　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''A Witch's Scientific Repose'' completed

### Head to the Vortex of Genesis

> *The battle with Nikador ends with the Chrysos Heirs' victory. We return to Okhema to rest and recover. Together, we will witness the return of the Titan's Coreflame and the start of the Coreflame's trial.*

Within Amphoreus, at (Trailblazer)'s private bath chamber...

**Mem:** Good thing you're finally up! Or that Giant's light is going to give you a sunburn!

**Dan Heng:** They've been this excited for a while now... What did you feed them?

- ▸ More like they snuck food themself.

**Mem:** Hmph, really now? I wouldn't do something like that!

**Mem:** Hmm... but come to think of it, I don't remember when the last time I ate something was...

- ▸ They don't seem to need food.

**Mem:** Says who! I'm still a... a cute and tiny animal, alright!

**Mem:** Hmm? But come to think of it... I don't remember when the last time I ate something was...

**Dan Heng:** ...Forget it. You can skip the translation. I can somehow tell that none of that chatter's meaningful.

**Dan Heng:** It's almost time. The Chrysos Heirs should be finishing up their preparations at the Vortex of Genesis.

**Dan Heng:** Let's go witness the Coreflame's return when you're ready.

### Wait for Phainon to start the Coreflame's trial

*(Head up the stairs towards the basin)*

**Trinnon:** "O majesty of the twelve Titans, pillars of the world—"

> **缇宁：**「庄严的十二泰坦，支撑世界的支柱——」

**Trinnon:** "We seek your divinity, to mend the rifts of the world—"

> **缇宁：**「我们于此索求神性，以填补世界的裂缝——」

**Trinnon:** "Fill our bodies with blood of gold, till we wither in willing service to the prophecy..."

> **缇宁：**「为肉身灌注黄金之血，为神谕甘愿枯竭干涸……」

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Aglaea:** Your guests are here, Phainon. We can begin the ritual now.

> **阿格莱雅：**你邀请的见证人到了，白厄。归还仪式可以开始了。

**Castorice:** Lord Mydei isn't here yet...

> **遐蝶：**万敌阁下没有到场……

**Phainon:** It's fine, Miss Castorice. He spoke to me already.

> **白厄：**他已经提前打过招呼了，遐蝶小姐。

**Phainon:** I told him that he should be nursing his wounds, and that he shouldn't be worrying about all this comrade's once-in-a-lifetime ascension ritual stuff.

> **白厄：**我告诉他，养好伤才是头等大事——好战友一生只会经历一次的晋升仪式什么的，不用太放在心上。

**Phainon:** So, I'm ready to begin.

> **白厄：**我准备好了。

**Aglaea:** Teacher, show him the way forward.

> **阿格莱雅：**吾师…请为他展现前路吧。

**Trinnon:** Despite the myriad dark and endless nights that have gone by, the guidance of the prophecy is nevertheless as clear and bright as the first ray of dawn.

> **缇宁：**哪怕在无边的幽暗中沉浸了万余个夜晚，神谕的指引也依旧如第一缕晨光般明亮，清晰。

**Trinnon:** Overthrow the gods, it declared.

> **缇宁：**它说：「倾覆诸神吧」。

**Trinnon:** Restore the Coreflames, it declared.

> **缇宁：**它说：「归还火种吧」。

**Trinnon:** Bear the divine authority, it declared.

> **缇宁：**它说：「承载神权吧」。

**Trinnon:** Forge miracles, it declared.

> **缇宁：**它说：「铸造奇迹吧」。

**Trinnon:** O prophecy, forgive us for this late tribute. We Chrysos Heirs have striven to start the Flame-Chase Journey anew over these myriad nights. The seeds we sowed have finally yielded a good harvest.

> **缇宁：**神谕，请宽赦这迟来的献礼。漫长的万余个黑夜里，我等黄金裔竭尽全力重启「逐火之旅」，如今，撒下的种子终于迎来丰收。

**Trinnon:** The seventh Coreflame needed for the miracle, that once belonged to the great Lance of Fury, Nikador, shall now be returned by an elected hero of the Chrysos Heirs: an upstanding citizen, a valiant warrior, and a noble soul.

> **缇宁：**奇迹渴慕的第七枚火种，曾属于伟大的「天谴之矛，尼卡多利」。而于此交还它的，是我等黄金裔推举出的英雄：一位正直的公民，一名骁勇的战士，一个高尚的灵魂。

**Trinnon:** Please step forward, Phainon of Aedes Elysiae.

> **缇宁：**哀丽秘榭的白厄——请上前来。

**Trinnon:** Recite with us:

> **缇宁：**与*我们*一起——

**Trinnon & Phainon:** "O majesty of the twelve Titans, pillars of the world—"

> **缇宁&白厄：**「庄严的十二泰坦，支撑世界的支柱——」

**Trinnon & Phainon:** "We seek your divinity, to mend the rifts of the world—"

> **缇宁&白厄：**「我们于此索求神性，以填补世界的裂缝——」

**Trinnon & Phainon:** "Fill our bodies with blood of gold, till we wither in willing service to the prophecy..."

> **缇宁&白厄：**「为肉身灌注黄金之血，为神谕甘愿枯竭干涸……」

**Trinnon:** Surrender the Coreflame, Chrysos Heir.

> **缇宁：**献上「火种」吧，黄金裔。

**Phainon:** The Lance of Fury's constellation is illuminated...

> **白厄：**「天谴之矛」的星辰被点亮了……

**Phainon:** Why is the Vortex this silent? Was it also like that in the past?

> **白厄：**为什么涡心如此沉默？你们也面临过类似的处境吗？

**Aglaea:** Patience, Phainon. Do not make us question this decision.

> **阿格莱雅：**你太心急了，白厄。不要让我们质疑这个决定。

**Castorice:** Lord... Gnaeus?

> **遐蝶：**格奈乌斯…阁下？

**Trinnon:** I'm afraid he is not the person you know, Castorice. That is a manifestation of the prophecy, an echo of the Titan's divinity. It is here to examine the Titan's successor.

> **缇宁：**恐怕他并非你认识的人，遐蝶——那是神谕的化身，泰坦神性的回响。它是来检视接替神权的黄金裔的。

**Divine Echo:** (Titan's murmurs)

> **神性的回响：**（泰坦的呓语）

**Trinnon:** "Mortal who wishes to inherit my divine authority..."

> **缇宁：**「意图接过神明权柄的凡人……」

**Trinnon:** "Your strength and character have been thoroughly tested..."

> **缇宁：**「你的力量与品格均已经受了考验……」

**Divine Echo:** (Titan's murmurs)

> **神性的回响：**（泰坦的呓语）

**Trinnon:** "Yet your will wavers..."

> **缇宁：**「但你的意志仍在动摇……」

**Trinnon:** "If you are to inherit Nikador's relentless fight against the unspeakable black tide..."

> **缇宁：**「若要延续尼卡多利的抗争，与不可名状的黑潮对抗……」

**Divine Echo:** (Titan's murmurs)

> **神性的回响：**（泰坦的呓语）

**Trinnon:** "You must accept a trial of divinity..."

> **缇宁：**「你必须接受神性的试炼……」

**Trinnon:** "Prove that you possess an indestructible will... And vanquish the fears deep in your heart..."

> **缇宁：**「证明你拥有无法被摧毁的意志…击溃你内心最根深蒂固的恐惧……」

**Phainon:** Vanquish... the fears in my heart?

> **白厄：**击溃…我内心的恐惧？

**Phainon:** So be it. No matter what obstacles you plan to put in my way, I'm ready to overcome them.

> **白厄：**那就来吧——不管你打算用什么方式阻挠我前进，我都已经做好准备了。

**Phainon:** For our prophesied tomorrow, I will become the god Amphoreus needs.

> **白厄：**为了预言中的明天，我会成为翁法罗斯需要的神。

**Divine Echo:** (Titan's murmurs)

> **神性的回响：**（泰坦的呓语）

**Trinnon:** "Come with me, Phainon of Aedes Elysiae..."

> **缇宁：**「随我来吧，哀丽秘榭的白厄……」

**Phainon & Trinnon:** "...In fear, you shall abandon your mortal body, and in suffering, be reborn as a god."

> **白厄&缇宁：**「…你将在恐惧中抛却凡躯，然后自苦痛中重生为神。」

**Phainon:** Wait for my return, my friends.

> **白厄：**等我回来，朋友们。

**Castorice:** Lord Phainon... is gone?

> **遐蝶：**白厄阁下…离开了？

**Aglaea:** He has stepped onto the battlefield to fight a campaign that can only be won by him alone.

> **阿格莱雅：**他已经走上了战场，那是只能由他独自赢下的战役。

**Aglaea:** All we can do is wait... and have faith.

> **阿格莱雅：**我们能做的只有等待…还有相信。

### Chat with the Chrysos Heirs

**Dan Heng:** What a... mysterious ritual. Is there really nothing we can do but wait?

> **丹恒：**坐等被命运仲裁的感觉，我不喜欢。不知道在场的其他人会如何看待。

**Dan Heng:** I don't know about the rest of them, but I don't like to just sit by and wait for my fate to be decided.

> **丹恒：**她好像睡过去了…看来主持这场仪式的消耗不小。

*(Talk with Aglaea)*

**Aglaea:** Phainon steers his own fate.

> **阿格莱雅：**白厄的命运由他自己掌舵。

**Aglaea:** If he can conquer his fear and return as a demigod, we will be one step closer to the miracle that the prophecy promised.

> **阿格莱雅：**若他能战胜自己的恐惧，以半神的姿态归来，我们距离神谕承诺的奇迹将无比接近。

- ▸ You have a lot of faith in him.

> ▸ 你好像有些忧心忡忡。

- ▸ What if he fails?

> ▸ 你们聊过了？

**Aglaea:** ...That would mean his time hasn't come yet. And that there is a more arduous mission for him to see through.

> **阿格莱雅：**…那代表他的时机未到，还有更艰巨的使命需要他去背负。

**Aglaea:** You must have noticed by now. Every Chrysos Heir here bears... a flaw of sorts. Be it the curse of immortality, or the constant erosion of their humanity, or...

> **阿格莱雅：**你应该已经注意到了，这里的每一位黄金裔都背负着某种…缺陷。或是不死的诅咒，或是不断流失的人性，或是……

**Aglaea:** But not Phainon. He is a Chrysos Heir without flaw, a perfect vessel for divinity.

> **阿格莱雅：**…但白厄不同。他是没有缺陷的黄金裔，完美的神性容器。

**Aglaea:** There will come a day when he leads everyone — everyone we have left — through the gates of the miracle.

> **阿格莱雅：**终有一日…他会带领所有人——剩下的人——敲开奇迹的大门。

*(Talk with Aglaea again, optional)*

**Aglaea:** Phainon steers his own fate.

> **阿格莱雅：**白厄的命运由他自己掌舵。

**Aglaea:** There will come a day when he leads everyone — everyone we have left — through the gates of the miracle.

> **阿格莱雅：**终有一日…他会带领所有人——剩下的人——敲开奇迹的大门。

**Trinnon:** Zzz... Zzz...

**Trinnon:** Snowy... zzz... will succeed...

**Castorice:** I pray you truly are prepared, Lord Phainon.

> ▸ 你对他真的很有信心。

- ▸ You spoke to him?

> ▸ 如果他失败了呢？

**Castorice:** Yes, there was hesitation in his words.

**Castorice:** The manifestation of the prophecy must have seen this. It saw Lord Phainon's heart wavering.

**Castorice:** Wherever he is, whatever he is fighting, I pray he makes it through this test.

*(Talk with Castorice again, optional)*

**Castorice:** Wherever he is, whatever he is fighting, I pray he makes it through this test.

*(After speaking with Aglaea)*

**Mydei:** Still too late, huh...

> **万敌：**还是晚来了一步么……

- ▸ Oops, missed the party...

> ▸ 这下没吃上热乎的。

- ▸ Phainon said you weren't coming.

> ▸ 白厄还说你不会来了。

> **万敌：**「天谴之矛」的星辰已经被点亮了，这意味着……

> **遐蝶：**火种的归还已经完成，但对白厄阁下的考验还没结束。

**Castorice:** The prophecy sent an ambassador. They acknowledged Lord Phainon's strength and character, but had doubts about his strength of will.

> **遐蝶：**神谕降下了它的代言人，它认可了白厄阁下的力量和品格，但对他的意志仍存疑虑。

**Castorice:** He must conquer his fear and prove himself to the prophecy.

> **遐蝶：**他必须战胜自己的恐惧…向神谕证明自己。

**Mydei:** ...I was right to be worried.

**Mydei:** I want to formally thank you, outsiders. Were it not for your aid, our campaign against Nikador would have been a hundred times more arduous.

> ▸ 不得表示表示？

**Mydei:** Kremnoans revere strength and military might, but that does not mean we lack etiquette.

> **万敌：**悬锋族人慕强、尚武，这不代表我们不懂得礼数。

> **丹恒：**万敌，你刚才说的「担忧」，指的是……

> **万敌：**那家伙，他始终都在被自己的过去折磨。

**Mydei:** I may not be the right person to tell you about him, but you must know his past if you are to continue fighting alongside Phainon.

> **万敌：**由我来传达或许不妥…但若要继续和白厄并肩战斗，你们有必要了解他的过去。

> **遐蝶：**在被神谕选中之前，白厄阁下已经被夺走了一切：故乡、家人、挚友…他曾誓言要守护的一切。

**Castorice:** He became a hollowed shell in his youth, and the blazing flames of vengeance were the only thing sustaining him.

> **遐蝶：**少年的他成了一副被挖空的躯壳，只有心中复仇的火焰驱使着他在大地上行走。

> **遐蝶：**神谕赋予了他新的使命，让他能以黄金裔的身份重启人生。但那新的使命和复仇的欲望相比，孰轻孰重？

> **万敌：**我认可他的力量，但恐惧——再利的剑也斩不断恐惧。

**Mydei:** ...Only a will as tough as stone can shatter the darkest nightmares.

Some system hours ago, aboard the Astral Express...

*(Mission completed)*

(Obtain a strand of Nameless Recollection for As I've Written)

Nameless Recollection

In that dawn-kissed city-state, a hero from the outlands became one of the Flame-Chasers. Inspired by the journey, they recorded their tales in the pages of a book.

(Unlock Achievement Generations Fall Like Leaves)

*(Investigate March 7th's bed on the Astral Express Passenger Cabin, optional)*

Curled up in the clear ice, the young girl remains silent with her eyes fixed shut.

Although the ice is not cold to the touch, there is something particularly saddening about seeing a young girl who is normally so full of life fall into complete silence.

A number of thin sheets of ice have shackled the unconscious March 7th to her bed. The once warm bedroom is now permeated with a bitter cold.

The young girl lays silent and flawless in the ice... Just as she did back when she was drifting through space.
